d:T473b7,<p>A mode expansion is nothing more than writing a vibrating object as a sum of standing waves. What makes the string interesting is that it lives in spacetime, so the amplitude of each harmonic is itself a spacetime vector <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>μ</mi></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_n^\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9114em;vertical-align:-0.247em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:-0.0037em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>. A gauge choice plus two constraints then strip the unphysical directions off those vectors, leaving only the transverse harmonics. Everything below is that stripping, done explicitly.</p>
<h2 id="step-1--the-equation-of-motion-is-just-the-wave-equation">Step 1 — The equation of motion is just the wave equation</h2>

<blockquote>
<p>This is the reward for the gauge work. The raw Nambu–Goto equations are nonlinear (a square root of derivatives sits in the action); the constraints <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mover accent="true"><mi>X</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mo>±</mo><msup><mi>X</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" rspace="0em">′</mo></msup><msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(\dot X\pm </blockquote>
<p>The general solution is any left-mover plus any right-mover, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math <h2 id="step-2--free-ends-pick-out-cosines">Step 2 — Free ends pick out cosines</h2>
<p>A space-filling D-brane leaves every endpoint free, so <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi mathvariant="script">P</mi><mrow><mi>σ</mi><mi>μ</mi></mrow></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mathcal P^{\sigma\mu}=0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord"><span style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> at <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>σ</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>π</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma=0,\pi</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span This is the payoff of choosing <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>σ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>π</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma\in[0,\pi]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.5782em;vertical-align:-0.0391em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">σ</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∈</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">π</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span> — the natural period drops out for free.</li>
</ul>
<p>A periodic <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>f</mi><mrow><mi>μ</mi><mo mathvariant="normal">′</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">f^{\mu\prime}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9463em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1076em;">f</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7519em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span><span class="mord mtight">′</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> is a Fourier series; integrating and collecting constants gives the conventional open-string expansion,</p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">X^\mu(\tau,\sigma) = x_0^\mu + 2\alpha' p^\mu\,\tau - i\sqrt{2\alpha'}\sum_{n\geq1}\frac{1}{\sqrt n}\big(a_n^{\mu*}e^{in\tau} - a_n^\mu e^{-in\tau}\big)\cos n\sigma. \tag{1}</span></p>
<blockquote>
<p>Term by term: <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mi>μ</mi></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_0^\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0486em;vertical-align:-0.2663em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7823em;"><span style="top:-2.4337em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.1809em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2663em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> is where the string sits; <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>α</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" rspace="0em">′</mo></msup><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>μ</mi></msup><mi>τ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">2\alpha' p^\mu\tau</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9463em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7519em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">′</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.1132em;">τ</span></span></span></span> is its uniform drift (the coefficient is fixed by <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>μ</mi></msup><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mo>∫</mo><mn>0</mn><mi>π</mi></msubsup><msup><mi mathvariant="script">P</mi><mrow><mi>τ</mi><mi>μ</mi></mrow></msup><mtext> </mtext><mi>d</mi><mi>σ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p^\mu=\int_0^\pi\mathcal P^{\tau\mu}\,d\sigma</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8588em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.2151em;vertical-align:-0.3558em;"></span><span class="mop"><span class="mop op-symbol small-op" mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">π</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>σ</mi></mrow><annotation standing-wave shape — antinodes at both ends. Kill every <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>μ</mi></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_n^\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9114em;vertical-align:-0.247em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> and the string shrinks to a point particle coasting along.</p>
</blockquote>
<h2 id="step-3--the-alpha--oscillator-notation">Step 3 — The <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>α</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span></span></span></span>-oscillator notation</h2>
<p>Two changes of variable turn (1) into the standard oscillator form: absorb <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msqrt><mi>n</mi></msqrt></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sqrt n</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.04em;vertical-align:-0.2397em;"></span><span class="mord sqrt"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8003em;"><span class="svg-align" style="top:-3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="padding-left:0.833em;">n</span></span><span style="top:-2.7603em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="hide-tail" style="min-width:0.853em;height:1.08em;"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="400em" height="1.08em" viewBox="0 0 400000 1080" preserveAspectRatio="xMinYMin slice"><path d="M95,702

<p>so reality of <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mi>μ</mi></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> becomes the single condition <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>α</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>n</mi></mrow><mi>μ</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><msubsup><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>μ</mi></msubsup><msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>∗</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha_{-n}^\mu=(\alpha_n^\mu)^*</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0642em;vertical-align:-0.2819em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7823em;"><span style="top:-2.4765em;margin-left:-0.0037em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">−</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span></span><span style="top:-3.1809em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:-0.0037em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose"><span class="mclose">)</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6887em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">∗</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>. The expansion and its two most useful derivatives are then</p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">X^\mu(\tau,\sigma) = x_0^\mu + \sqrt{2\alpha'}\,\alpha_0^\mu\,\tau + i\sqrt{2\alpha'}\sum_{n\neq0}\frac{1}{n}\,\alpha_n^\mu\,e^{-in\tau}\cos n\sigma, \tag{2}</span></p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">\boxed{\;\dot X^\mu \pm X^{\mu\prime} = \sqrt{2\alpha'}\sum_{n\in\mathbb Z}\alpha_n^\mu\,e^{-in(\tau\pm\sigma)}\;}. \tag{3}</span></p>
<blockquote>
<p>Equation (3) is the workhorse. The constraint we must impose is built from precisely <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mover accent="true"><mi>X</mi><mo>˙</mo></mover><mi>μ</mi></msup><mo>±</mo><msup><mi>X</mi><mrow><mi>μ</mi><mo mathvariant="normal">′</mo></mrow></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\dot X^\mu\pm X^{\mu\prime}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0035em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord accent"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.9202em;"><span style="top:-3em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span></span><span style="top:-3.2523em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:3em;"></span><span class="accent-body" style="left:-0.0556em;"><span class="mord">˙</span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.6644em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">±</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7519em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7519em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">μ</span><span class="mord mtight">′</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>, and written as a single exponential sum it is diagonal in the mode label — so imposing the constraint will reduce to matching Fourier coefficients, one <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>n</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span></span></span></span> at a time.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>Everything so far holds for every <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span></span></span></span>, in any gauge of this class. The light-cone choice now decides which of these modes are physically free.</p>
<h2 id="step-4--impose-light-cone-gauge">Step 4 — Impose light-cone gauge</h2>
<p>From <a href="/rome-wiki/articles/string-theory/part-ii-classical-strings/12-classical-light-cone-gauge/fixing-x-plus-as-worldsheet-time/" class="wiki-link">Fixing X-plus as worldsheet time</a>, the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>+</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">+</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">+</span></span></span></span> direction is frozen into a clock — no position zero mode, no oscillators:</p>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>=</mo><msqrt><mrow><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>α</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" mathvariant="normal">≠</mo><mn>0</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mi mathvariant="normal">.</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^+ = \sqrt{2\alpha'}\,\alpha_0^+\tau = 2\alpha' p^+\tau, <p>The transverse directions <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mi>I</mi></msup></mrow><annotation class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>…</mo><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">I=2,\dots,D-1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8778em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="minner">…</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, so <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D-2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> of them) are the free physical fields — just (2) with <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi><mo>→</mo><mi>I</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu\to I</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span>:</p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">\boxed{\;X^I(\tau,\sigma) = x_0^I + \sqrt{2\alpha'}\,\alpha_0^I\,\tau + i\sqrt{2\alpha'}\sum_{n\neq0}\frac{1}{n}\,\alpha_n^I\,e^{-in\tau}\cos n\sigma,\qquad \alpha_0^I = \sqrt{2\alpha'}\,p^I\;}. \tag{4}</span></p>
<p>This is the open-string light-cone solution for the physical degrees of freedom. The only free data at this point are the transverse constants <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mi>I</mi></msubsup><mo separator="true">,</mo><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>I</mi></msup><mo separator="true">,</mo><msubsup><mi>α</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>I</mi></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_0^I,p^I,\alpha_n^I</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0894em;vertical-align:-0.2481em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-2.4519em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2481em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:-0.0037em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> plus the two longitudinal numbers <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p^+</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9658em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">+</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_0^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.1031em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 The last step <em>computes</em> the remaining longitudinal motion — it adds no new freedom.</p>
<h2 id="step-5--the-constraint-fixes-the-x--modes">Step 5 — The constraint fixes the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> modes</h2>
<p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> obeys the same wave equation and Neumann conditions, so it has the same expansion (2) with <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>μ</mi><mo>→</mo><mo>−</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\mu\to-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.625em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">μ</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">→</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">−</span></span></span></span>. To pin its modes, impose the solved constraint from <a href="/rome-wiki/articles/string-theory/part-ii-classical-strings/12-classical-light-cone-gauge/light-cone-constraints/" class="wiki-link">Light-cone constraints</a>,</p>

<p>and expand both sides using (3):</p>

Track the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>α</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" rspace="0em">′</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\alpha'</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7519em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7519em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">′</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> carefully here: the leftover <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">1/(2p^+)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0213em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">1/</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">+</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> is what turns into the transverse Virasoro mode below.</p>

<p>Relabel <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>q</mi><mo>=</mo><mi>n</mi><mo>−</mo><mi>p</mi></mrow><annotation style="margin-right:0.1132em;">τ</span><span class="mbin mtight">±</span><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">σ</span><span class="mclose mtight">)</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>:</p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">\boxed{\;\sqrt{2\alpha'}\,\alpha_n^- = \frac{1}{2p^+}\sum_{p\in\mathbb Z}\alpha_{n-p}^I\alpha_p^I = \frac{1}{p^+}\,L_n^\perp\;}, \qquad L_n^\perp \equiv \frac12\sum_{p\in\mathbb Z}\alpha_{n-p}^I\alpha_p^I. \tag{5}</span></p>
<blockquote>
<p>Every <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> oscillator is a <strong>quadratic in the transverse oscillators</strong> — the <em>transverse Virasoro mode</em> <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>L</mi><mi>n</mi><mo>⊥</mo></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">L_n^\perp</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0961em;vertical-align:-0.247em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">L</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8491em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mrel mtight">⊥</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>. The spacetime index on the right runs over transverse labels only: that is what "<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> is a function of the physical data" means concretely (see <a href="/rome-wiki/articles/string-theory/part-ii-classical-strings/12-classical-light-cone-gauge/transverse-coordinates-as-physical-degrees-of-freedom/" class="wiki-link">Transverse coordinates as physical degrees of freedom</a>). Only <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">x_0^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0778em;vertical-align:-0.2663em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8115em;"><span style="top:-2.4337em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.1031em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2663em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> stays free.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>Substituting (5) into the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> expansion gives <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> explicitly in terms of the physical modes:</p>
<span class="katex-display"><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML" display="block"><semantics><mtable width="100%"><mtr><mtd width="50%"></mtd><mtd><mrow><menclose notation="box"><mstyle scriptlevel="0" displaystyle="false"><mstyle scriptlevel="0" displaystyle="false"><mstyle scriptlevel="0" displaystyle="true"><mrow><mtext>  </mtext><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>τ</mi><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>σ</mi><mo stretchy="false">)</mo><mo>=</mo><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msubsup><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mn>1</mn><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfrac><msubsup><mi>L</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>⊥</mo></msubsup><mtext> </mtext><mi>τ</mi><mo>+</mo><mfrac><mi>i</mi><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup></mfrac><munder><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo mathvariant="normal">≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow></munder><mfrac><mn>1</mn><mi>n</mi></mfrac><msubsup><mi>L</mi><mi>n</mi><mo>⊥</mo></msubsup><msup><mi>e</mi><mrow><mo>−</mo><mi>i</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>τ</mi></mrow></msup><mi>cos</mi><mo>⁡</mo><mi>n</mi><mi>σ</mi><mtext>  </mtext></mrow></mstyle></mstyle></mstyle></menclose><mi mathvariant="normal">.</mi></mrow></mtd><mtd width="50%"></mtd><mtd><mtext>(6)</mtext></mtd></mtr></mtable><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\boxed{\;
X^-(\tau,\sigma)
=x_0^-+\frac{1}{p^+}L_0^\perp\,\tau
+\frac{i}{p^+}\sum_{n\neq0}\frac{1}{n}L_n^\perp e^{-in\tau}\cos n\sigma
\;}. \tag{6}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:3.4547em;vertical-align:-1.7782em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:1.6765em;"><span style="top:-5.4547em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:5.4547em;"></span><span class="boxpad"><span class="mord"><span class="mord"><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:1.7782em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">.</span></span><span class="tag"><span class="strut" style="height:3.4547em;vertical-align:-1.7782em;"></span><span class="mord text"><span class="mord">(</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">6</span></span><span class="mord">)</span></span></span></span></span></span>
<blockquote>
<p>Choose the transverse oscillators, form the quadratics <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>L</mi><mi>n</mi><mo>⊥</mo></msubsup></mrow><annotation class="vlist" style="height:0.8491em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mrel mtight">⊥</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span>, and <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>X</mi><mo>−</mo></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">X^-</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7713em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">X</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> is written out — every non-constant piece is fixed, with <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>x</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>−</mo></msubsup></mrow><annotation style="height:1.0778em;vertical-align:-0.2663em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">x</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8115em;"><span style="top:-2.4337em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">0</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.1031em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2663em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> the single leftover integration constant.</p>
</blockquote>
<p>So the complete open-string solution is an algorithm, not a search:</p>
<ol>
<li>Pick <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><mo>></mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">p^+>0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.9658em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">p</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7713em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">+</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">></span><span class="mspace" <p>No longitudinal oscillator is ever chosen independently — that is the whole content of "the transverse modes are the physical degrees of freedom."</p>
<h2 id="step-6--the-mass-is-pure-transverse-energy">Step 6 — The mass is pure transverse energy</h2>
<p>The <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation <p><span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>L</mi><mn>0</mn><mo>⊥</mo></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mstyle scriptlevel="0" mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" rspace="0em">′</mo></msup><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>I</mi></msup><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>I</mi></msup><mo>+</mo><msub><mo>∑</mo><mrow><mi>n</mi><mo>≥</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow></msub><mi>n</mi><mtext> </mtext><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>∗</mo></mrow></msubsup><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>I</mi></msubsup><mi mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span><span class="mord mtight">∗</span></span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord">.</span></span></span></span></p>
<p>Feed this into <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><msup><mi>p</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>+</mo></msup><msup><mi>p</mi><mo>−</mo></msup><mo>−</mo><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>I</mi></msup><msup><mi>p</mi><mi>I</mi></msup></mrow><annotation style="height:0.8141em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.109em;">M</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span mtight"><span class="mbin mtight">−</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> (mostly-plus signature); the <span reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> terms cancel:</p>
<p><span class="katex-error" title="ParseError: KaTeX parse error: \tag works only in display equations" style="color:#cc0000">\boxed{\;M^2 = \frac{1}{\alpha'}\sum_{n\geq1} n\,a_n^{I*}\,a_n^I\;}. \tag{7}</span></p>
<blockquote>
<p>A string's mass is the energy stored in its transverse vibrations — nothing else. Each term is <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>n</mi><mtext> </mtext><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>I</mi></msubsup><msup><mi mathvariant="normal">∣</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>≥</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">n\,|a_n^I|^2\geq0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0913em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">n</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-2.453em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight">n</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.247em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord">∣</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">≥</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>, so <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>≥</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">≥</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>: <strong>the classical string is never a tachyon</strong>, and a non-vibrating string (<span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mi>n</mi><mi>I</mi></msubsup><mo>=</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation See <a href="/rome-wiki/articles/string-theory/part-ii-classical-strings/08-relativistic-point-particle/mass-shell-relation/" class="wiki-link">Mass-shell relation</a>.</p>

<aside class="callout callout-warning">
<p class="callout-title">Warning</p>
<p>This is the <em>classical</em> mass, and it does not survive quantization.

style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, which pushes the ground state to <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn><mi mathvariant="normal">/</mi><msup><mi>α</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" rspace="0em">′</mo></msup><mo>&#x3C;</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">M^2=-1/\alpha'&#x3C;0</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8141em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.109em;">M</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0019em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1/</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0037em;">α</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.7519em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight"><span class="mord mtight">′</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">&#x3C;</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span> — a genuine tachyon — and discretizes the spectrum. Derived in Part III.</p>
</aside>
<h2 id="static-gauge-vs-light-cone-gauge">Static gauge vs light-cone gauge</h2>
<p>Both gauges freeze one coordinate (the static-gauge column keeps <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>c</mi></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">c</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">c</span></span></span></span>, as Module 11 did). The difference is whether the leftover constraints can be <em>solved</em> or only <em>imposed</em>.</p>

<td>Can you <em>solve</em> them?</td>
<td><strong>No</strong> — only impose</td>

<blockquote>

In light-cone gauge the cross term <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><msup><mover </blockquote>
<h2 id="open-questions">Open questions</h2>
<ul>
<li>The closed string is not done here: it uses <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>β</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\beta=1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8889em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0528em;">β</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>σ</mi><mo>∈</mo><mo stretchy="false">[</mo><mn>0</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mn>2</mn><mi>π</mi><mo stretchy="false">]</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\sigma\in[0,2\pi]</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.5782em;vertical-align:-0.0391em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">σ</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">∈</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">[</span><span class="mord">0</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0359em;">π</span><span class="mclose">]</span></span></span></span>, <strong>independent</strong> left- and right-moving oscillators, and a global level-matching condition. Deferred to the closed-string modules.</li>
<li>The critical dimension <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>26</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D=26</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">26</span></span></span></span> and the constant <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>a</mi><mo>=</mo><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a=-1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">−</span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span> are asserted above, not derived. The derivation — demanding the hidden Lorentz generators close under quantization — belongs to the light-cone quantization notes in Part III.</li>
</ul>
<h2 id="exercise">Exercise</h2>
<p>The mass formula (7) fixes <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">M^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8141em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.109em;">M</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> but says nothing about <strong>spin</strong>. Take the first excited open string: excite only the lowest mode, in a single transverse direction, so <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>2</mn></msubsup><mo mathvariant="normal">≠</mo><mn>0</mn></mrow><annotation <p><strong>(a)</strong> Compute <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>α</mi><mo mathvariant="normal" lspace="0em" class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7278em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">+</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.4306em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">0</span></span></span></span>: it becomes massless. But that is Part III; here <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msup><mi>M</mi><mn>2</mn></msup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">M^2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8141em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.109em;">M</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">2</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> is whatever the classical amplitude makes it.)</p>
<p><strong>(b)</strong> The excitation carries the transverse index <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">I=2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span>: it points along a specific direction in the <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">(D-2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>-dimensional transverse space. So it is <strong>not a scalar</strong> — it transforms as a <em>vector</em> under <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>S</mi><mi>O</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">SO(D-2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">S</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span> rotations of the transverse space. Exciting <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn><mn>3</mn></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_1^3</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0622em;vertical-align:-0.2481em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8141em;"><span style="top:-2.4519em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">3</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2481em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> instead would give a rotated partner; the set <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mo stretchy="false">{</mo><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn><mi>I</mi></msubsup><mo stretchy="false">}</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">\{a_1^I\}</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0913em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mopen">{</span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-2.4519em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2481em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span class="mclose">}</span></span></span></span>, <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>I</mi><mo>=</mo><mn>2</mn><mo separator="true">,</mo><mo>…</mo><mo separator="true">,</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>1</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">I=2,\dots,D-1</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span><span class="mrel">=</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2778em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.8778em;vertical-align:-0.1944em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="minner">…</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mpunct">,</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.1667em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">1</span></span></span></span>, rotates into itself.</p>
<p><strong>(c)</strong> The distinct single-oscillator excitations <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><msubsup><mi>a</mi><mn>1</mn><mi>I</mi></msubsup></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">a_1^I</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1.0894em;vertical-align:-0.2481em;"></span><span class="mord"><span class="mord mathnormal">a</span><span class="msupsub"><span class="vlist-t vlist-t2"><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.8413em;"><span style="top:-2.4519em;margin-left:0em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mtight">1</span></span></span><span style="top:-3.063em;margin-right:0.05em;"><span class="pstrut" style="height:2.7em;"></span><span class="sizing reset-size6 size3 mtight"><span class="mord mathnormal mtight" style="margin-right:0.0785em;">I</span></span></span></span><span class="vlist-s">​</span></span><span class="vlist-r"><span class="vlist" style="height:0.2481em;"><span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span> come in exactly <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D-2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> flavors, one per transverse direction, forming a vector of <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>S</mi><mi>O</mi><mo stretchy="false">(</mo><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn><mo stretchy="false">)</mo></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">SO(D-2)</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0576em;">S</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">O</span><span class="mopen">(</span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:1em;vertical-align:-0.25em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span><span class="mclose">)</span></span></span></span>. A massless vector particle is precisely a <strong>photon</strong> (a massless spin-1 gauge boson has <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D-2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> physical polarizations). So the open string's lowest nontrivial excitation is a candidate photon — the transverse count <span class="katex"><span class="katex-mathml"><math xmlns="http://www.w3.org/1998/Math/MathML"><semantics><mrow><mi>D</mi><mo>−</mo><mn>2</mn></mrow><annotation encoding="application/x-tex">D-2</annotation></semantics></math></span><span class="katex-html" aria-hidden="true"><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.7667em;vertical-align:-0.0833em;"></span><span class="mord mathnormal" style="margin-right:0.0278em;">D</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span><span class="mbin">−</span><span class="mspace" style="margin-right:0.2222em;"></span></span><span class="base"><span class="strut" style="height:0.6444em;"></span><span class="mord">2</span></span></span></span> is what makes the polarization bookkeeping come out right. This is why the "physical = transverse" logic of light-cone gauge is not just tidy but predictive: it hands you the right number of photon polarizations automatically.</p>
